test_zenith_style_consumer_still_parses_headless_json_after_action_logging(tmp_path):",
  "docs/features/25_issue-84_cli-contract-support-stable-headless-project-selection-c8d4bcc1/sbe.md": "diff --git a/docs/features/25_issue-84_cli-contract-support-stable-headless-project-selection-c8d4bcc1/sbe.md b/docs/features/25_issue-84_cli-contract-support-stable-headless-project-selection-c8d4bcc1/sbe.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..10322f6\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/docs/features/25_issue-84_cli-contract-support-stable-headless-project-selection-c8d4bcc1/sbe.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,84 @@\n+# SBE (Specification by Example) Template\n+\n+> 📅 Created: 2026-04-24\n+> 🔗 Issue: https://github.com/oatrice/Luma/issues/84\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Feature: Stable headless project selection and explicit resolved-target reporting\n+\n+ฟีเจอร์นี้ทำให้ Luma headless contract รับค่า `--project` ที่เสถียรกว่า numeric key อย่างเดียว โดยรองรับ repo, path, slug หรือ equivalent durable selector ที่ resolve ได้อย่าง unique และคืน `resolved_target` กลับมาใน JSON response เพื่อให้ external callers ตรวจได้ว่า action รันกับ target ไหนจริง\n+\n+### Scenario: Stable selector resolves the intended target - Happy Path\n+\n+**Given** caller ส่ง explicit selector ผ่าน `--project`\n+**When** selector นั้น map ไปยัง local target ได้อย่าง unique\n+**Then** Luma ต้องรัน action กับ target นั้น และคืน `resolved_target` ที่ระบุ repo/path/key/slug ตามที่ resolve ได้จริง\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| action | `--project` value | current cwd | expected resolved target |\n+|--------|-------------------|-------------|--------------------------|\n+| `code_review` | `oatrice/Luma` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | `repo=oatrice/Luma`, `project_key=12`, `slug=luma` |\n+| `code_review` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Cerebro` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | `repo=oatrice/Cerebro`, `project_key=13`, `slug=cerebro` |\n+| `bootstrap` | `zenith` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Zenith` | `repo=oatrice/Zenith`, `project_key=null`, `slug=zenith` |\n+| `create_issue` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Cerebro` | `repo=oatrice/Luma`, `project_key=12`, `slug=luma` |\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Scenario: Invalid or ambiguous selector fails loudly - Edge Case / Error Handling\n+\n+**Given** caller ส่ง selector ที่ไม่ unique หรือไม่สามารถ map ไป local target ได้อย่างปลอดภัย\n+**When** Luma พยายาม resolve `--project`\n+**Then** ระบบต้องคืน machine-readable error และห้าม fallback ไป project อื่นแบบเงียบ ๆ\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| `--project` value | expected error |\n+|-------------------|----------------|\n+| `backend` | `Ambiguous project selector 'backend'.` |\n+| `oatrice/UnknownRepo` | `Could not resolve project selector 'oatrice/UnknownRepo'.` |\n+| `/tmp/not-a-repo` | `Unknown project key or invalid path '/tmp/not-a-repo'.` |\n+| `zenith` | `Could not resolve project selector 'zenith'.` เมื่อไม่มี unique local mapping |\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Scenario: Explicit selector precedence beats fragile context - Boundary Conditions\n+\n+**Given** environment มี stored project หรือ cwd inference ที่อาจชี้ไปคนละ repo\n+**When** caller ส่ง explicit `--project`\n+**Then** explicit selector ต้องชนะ context อื่นเสมอ และถ้าไม่มี explicit selector จึงค่อยใช้ legacy fallback ตามลำดับ\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| `--project` value | stored project | current cwd | expected precedence result |\n+|-------------------|----------------|-------------|----------------------------|\n+| `oatrice/Zenith` | `1` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | resolve ไป `oatrice/Zenith` |\n+| `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Cerebro` | `12` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | resolve ไป `oatrice/Cerebro` |\n+| `12` | `1` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | resolve ไป key `12` |\n+| `not provided` | `13` | `/Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Luma-worktrees/luma1` | fallback ไป stored project `13` จนกว่าจะมี better explicit selector |\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Scenario: Bootstrap keeps legacy compatibility while reporting the resolved target - Boundary Conditions\n+\n+**Given** `bootstrap` เป็น headless action เดิมที่ downstream automation ใช้อยู่แล้ว\n+**When** caller รัน `bootstrap` ด้วย numeric key หรือ stable selector\n+**Then** Luma ต้องยัง bootstrap branch/state ได้ตามเดิม และ JSON ต้องมี `resolved_target` เพื่อให้ caller audit target ได้\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| command shape | expected |\n+|---------------|----------|\n+| `--auto --action bootstrap --issue 84 --project 12 --json` | success JSON, `resolved_target.project_key=\"12\"` |\n+| `--auto --action bootstrap --issue 36 --project /Users/oatrice/Software-projects/Zenith --json` | success JSON, `resolved_target.repo=\"oatrice/Zenith\"` |\n+| `--auto --action bootstrap --issue 36 --project oatrice/Zenith --json` | success JSON พร้อม `resolved_target` หรือ explicit JSON error ถ้า local mapping ยังไม่พร้อม |\n+| `--auto --action bootstrap --issue 84 --project backend --json` | JSON error, ไม่มี silent fallback ไป JarWise หรือ Luma |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+- `#84` ควรถูกมองเป็น standalone contract fix โดยใช้ implementation เดิมจาก `#40` เป็น baseline compatibility\n+- ถ้าหลังจากเพิ่ม `resolved_target` แล้วยังต้องการ rich bootstrap payload หรือ interactive parity เพิ่มเติม ให้เปิด follow-up issue แยก แทนการ inflate `#84`\n+- งาน `#43` (Telegram step-progress) และ `#44` (doc-quality validation) อยู่คนนละ concern และไม่ควรถูกรวมเข้ามาใน scope นี้",
  "docs/features/17_issue-40-42-41_add-headless-select-issue-action-that-mirrors-menu-000930f1/spec.md": "diff --git a/docs/features/17_issue-40-42-41_add-headless-select-issue-action-that-mirrors-menu-000930f1/spec.md b/docs/features/17_issue-40-42-41_add-headless-select-issue-action-that-mirrors-menu-000930f1/spec.md\nindex d594f7f..519f741 100644\n--- a/docs/features/17_issue-40-42-41_add-headless-select-issue-action-that-mirrors-menu-000930f1/spec.md\n+++ b/docs/features/17_issue-40-42-41_add-headless-select-issue-action-that-mirrors-menu-000930f1/spec.md\n@@ -1,81 +1,126 @@\n-# Specification: Headless CLI Expansion & First-Class Issue Management\n+# Specification Update: Existing Headless Bootstrap and Stable Selector Follow-up\n \n-> **Status**: Draft\n-> **Owner**: Expert Product Manager & Systems Analyst\n-> **Dates**: Created: 2026-04-06 | Last Updated: 2026-04-06\n+> อัปเดตล่าสุด: 2026-04-24\n+> สถานะ: Current-state aligned\n \n-## 1. Context & Goal\n-ปัจจุบัน Luma มีความสามารถในการทำงานแบบ Interactive ผ่าน Menu ต่างๆ ได้อย่างครบถ้วน แต่ในส่วนของ **Headless CLI Contract** (สำหรับการเรียกใช้งานจากภายนอกหรือ Automation) ยังรองรับเพียงแค่ `code_review` เท่านั้น ทำให้ไม่สามารถเริ่ม Workflow ใหม่ (Bootstrap), สร้าง Issue หรือรัน Workflow แบบเต็มรูปแบบ (Auto Full Workflow) ผ่านเครื่องมือภายนอกได้\n+## 1. Context\n \n-### Problem\n-1. **Issue #40**: การเลือก Issue และการสร้าง Branch (Menu 2) ผูกติดกับ Interactive UI เท่านั้น ทำให้ Automation ไม่สามารถตั้งค่าสถานะ (State) เริ่มต้นที่ถูกต้องได้\n-2. **Issue #42**: การสร้าง GitHub Issue ถูกซ่อนอยู่ในเมนู Update Roadmap ทำให้ค้นหาได้ยากและไม่สามารถสั่งงานแยกต่างหากผ่าน CLI ได้\n-3. **Issue #41**: Workflow แบบอัตโนมัติ (Auto Full Workflow - Menu A) ซึ่งเป็นจุดแข็งของ Luma ไม่สามารถสั่งการผ่าน Headless mode ได้ ทำให้การขยายผลไปใช้กับ CI/CD หรือ Agent ภายนอกทำได้ยาก\n+เดิม spec ชุดนี้ตั้งเป้าไว้กว้างสำหรับ `#40`, `#42`, และ `#41` พร้อมกัน แต่สถานะปัจจุบันเปลี่ยนไป:\n \n-### Goal\n-ขยายความสามารถของ Headless CLI ให้ครอบคลุมการทำงานหลักของ Luma ทั้งหมด ตั้งแต่การเลือก Issue (Bootstrap), การสร้าง Issue ใหม่แบบ First-class และการรัน Full Workflow แบบเก็บสถานะ (Resumable) โดยยังคงรักษามาตรฐาน JSON stdout contract ไว้\n+- `#40` มี implementation แล้วใน codebase ผ่าน action `bootstrap`\n+- ปัญหาเร่งด่วนของ ecosystem ตอนนี้อยู่ที่ project selection semantics ไม่เสถียร\n+- follow-up ที่สำคัญที่สุดคือ `#84`\n \n----\n+ดังนั้น spec update นี้มีจุดประสงค์เพื่อกำหนด “ขอบเขตจริง” ของสิ่งที่ต้องรักษาและสิ่งที่ควรทำต่อ โดยไม่ย้อนกลับไปมอง `#40` เป็นงานที่ยังว่างทั้งหมด\n \n-## 2. User Journey & Requirements\n+## 2. Current Contract Baseline\n \n-### User Story\n-As a **Developer/Automation Tool**, I want to **trigger Luma actions via machine-readable commands**, so that **I can integrate Luma's powerful workflow into my external scripts and CI pipelines without manual intervention**.\n+### 2.1 Existing Behavior That Must Be Preserved\n \n-### Functional Requirements\n-- [ ] **Headless Issue Selection (#40)**: เพิ่ม Action สำหรับเลือก Issue จาก Kanban และทำการ Bootstrap branch โดยอัตโนมัติ พร้อมคืนค่า JSON state\n-- [ ] **First-Class Issue Creation (#42)**: เพิ่มเมนูสำหรับสร้าง Issue โดยตรง (ทั้ง Interactive และ Headless) และต้องรองรับ Template ที่มีส่วน `## Related`\n-- [ ] **Headless Guided Workflow (#41)**: รองรับการรัน `Auto Full Workflow` ผ่าน Headless mode โดยมีการเก็บ Checkpoint เพื่อให้สามารถ Resume งานต่อได้หากเกิดการหยุดชะงัก\n-- [ ] **JSON Contract Consistency**: ผลลัพธ์ของ Headless actions ทั้งหมดต้องเป็น JSON ผ่าน STDOUT เท่านั้น (ห้ามมี Log อื่นปน)\n-- [ ] **Interactive Parity**: ฟีเจอร์ใหม่ในส่วน Interactive ต้องไม่ทำให้ Workflow เดิมถดถอย (No regressions)\n+Luma ปัจจุบันต้องยังคงรองรับ:\n \n-### Non-Functional Requirements\n-- **Reliability**: การเปลี่ยนสถานะ (State transitions) ใน Headless mode ต้องแม่นยำและบันทึกลง `.luma_state.json` เสมอ\n-- **Observability**: Headless output ต้องระบุชัดเจนว่าขณะนี้อยู่ใน Phase ใดของ Workflow\n+- headless action `bootstrap`\n+- การเลือก issue แบบระบุหมายเลข issue\n+- การ bootstrap branch\n+- การ transition state ไป `coding`\n+- การทำงานแบบ JSON stdout contract เมื่อใช้ `--json`\n \n----\n+### 2.2 Current Known Contract Gaps\n \n-## 3. Specification by Example (SBE)\n+สิ่งที่ยังไม่ถือว่า fully aligned:\n \n-### Scenario: Headless Issue Selection & Bootstrap (#40)\n-**Given** มี Issue #123 ในสถานะ 'Ready' บน GitHub Kanban\n-**When** เรียกใช้คำสั่ง headless ด้วย action `select_issue` และระบุหมายเลข issue\n-**Then** Luma ต้องสร้าง branch ตาม naming convention, อัปเดต state เป็น `coding` และคืนค่า JSON รายละเอียดของ issue และ branch\n+- response ของ `bootstrap` ยังไม่ให้ structured payload ที่ชัดเจนพอสำหรับ external callers\n+- ยังไม่ชัดว่าการเลือก issue ของ headless path บังคับ selection constraints เท่ากับ interactive path\n+- project resolution สำหรับ headless actions ยังพึ่ง numeric `--project` มากเกินไป\n \n-#### Examples\n-| Input (Action + Issue) | System State (Before) | Output (JSON) | Resulting Branch |\n-|-------------------------|-----------------------|---------------|------------------|\n-| `select_issue --id 123` | `idle`                | `{\"issue\": 123, \"branch\": \"feat/123-login\", \"status\": \"success\"}` | `feat/123-login` |\n-| `select_issue --id 999` | `idle` (Issue not found)| `{\"error\": \"Issue not found\", \"status\": \"failure\"}` | (No change) |\n+## 3. Scope of the Next Work\n \n-### Scenario: First-Class Issue Creation (#42)\n-**Given** ผู้ใช้ต้องการสร้าง Issue ใหม่สำหรับบั๊กที่พบ\n-**When** เรียกใช้เมนู `Create Issue` (Interactive) หรือ headless action `create_issue`\n-**Then** ระบบต้องสร้าง Issue บน GitHub โดยมีส่วนประกอบ `## Related` ใน Body และคืนค่า URL/Number กลับมา\n+### 3.1 In Scope\n \n-#### Examples\n-| Input (Title) | Body Contains | Result |\n-|---------------|---------------|--------|\n-| \"Fix UI bug\"  | `## Related: #10` | Created Issue #124 |\n-| \"New Feature\" | (Empty Related) | Created Issue #125 with placeholder `## Related` |\n+งานถัดไปที่ควรทำภายใต้ `#84` และต้อง compatible กับ implementation จาก `#40`:\n \n-### Scenario: Resuming Headless Guided Workflow (#41)\n-**Given** Workflow เคยรันค้างไว้ที่ Phase `planning`\n-**When** เรียกใช้ headless `auto_workflow` พร้อมส่ง Flag หรือ State เดิมกลับไป\n-**Then** ระบบต้องอ่าน Checkpoint และเริ่มทำงานต่อจากจุดเดิมโดยไม่เริ่มใหม่ตั้งแต่ต้น\n+- รองรับ stable selector สำหรับ headless actions เช่น:\n+  - `repo`\n+  - `path`\n+  - `slug`\n+  - หรือ durable identity ที่เทียบเท่า\n+- กำหนด precedence ของ project resolution ให้ deterministic\n+- ให้ machine-readable response echo resolved target กลับมาแบบ explicit\n+- ทำให้ `bootstrap` และ headless actions อื่นใช้ resolver เดียวกัน\n \n-#### Examples\n-| Current State (in json) | Command | Expected Output Phase |\n-|-------------------------|---------|-----------------------|\n-| `{\"phase\": \"coding\"}`   | `auto_workflow --resume` | `{\"phase\": \"reviewing\", ...}` |\n-| `{\"phase\": \"idle\"}`     | `auto_workflow` | `{\"phase\": \"selecting\", ...}` |\n+### 3.2 Out of Scope\n \n----\n+สำหรับรอบนี้ ไม่ควร drift ไปทำ:\n \n-## 4. Constraints & Risks\n-- **JSON STDOUT Conflict**: ต้องระวังไม่ให้ Python print ค่าอื่นๆ ออกมานอกจาก JSON เมื่ออยู่ในโหมด Headless (ต้องใช้ `RedirectStdout` หรือการจัดการ Logger ที่เข้มงวด)\n-- **State Lock**: การรัน Headless workflow หลายตัวพร้อมกันอาจทำให้ `.luma_state.json` เกิดการเขียนทับกันได้ (Race condition)\n-- **Interactive Prompts**: ในโหมด Headless หากโค้ดเดิมมีการเรียก `input()` จะทำให้โปรแกรมค้าง (Hanging) ต้องแน่ใจว่าได้จำลอง Input (Mock) หรือ Bypass ส่วนที่ต้องการ Interactive ทั้งหมดเมื่อรันแบบ Machine-readable\n+- first-class issue creation เต็มรูปแบบของ `#42`\n+- resumable guided workflow เต็มรูปแบบของ `#41`\n+- transcript / telemetry polish ที่ไม่จำเป็นต่อ selector correctness\n \n----\n-**Next Step**: เมื่อ Spec นี้ได้รับการอนุมัติ จะดำเนินการสร้าง `plan.md` เพื่อลงรายละเอียดการแก้ไขใน `main.py` และ `luma_core/actions/` ต่อไป\n\\ No newline at end of file\n+## 4. Functional Requirements\n+\n+### FR-1 Stable Selector Input\n+\n+headless caller ต้องสามารถระบุ target project โดยไม่ต้องพึ่ง numeric key อย่างเดียว\n+\n+ตัวอย่าง selector ที่ยอมรับได้:\n+\n+- `repo`\n+- `path`\n+- `slug`\n+- legacy `--project` numeric key ในฐานะ fallback\n+\n+### FR-2 Deterministic Resolution Precedence\n+\n+ระบบต้องกำหนด precedence ชัดเจนระหว่าง:\n+\n+1. explicit stable selector\n+2. explicit legacy `--project`\n+3. cwd / dynamic detection\n+4. stored last project\n+\n+### FR-3 Explicit Resolved Target in Machine-readable Output\n+\n+headless JSON response ต้องระบุ target ที่ระบบ resolve ได้จริง เช่น:\n+\n+- project key ที่ใช้\n+- repo ที่ resolve ได้\n+- path ที่ใช้จริง\n+- slug หรือ canonical identifier ที่เทียบได้\n+\n+### FR-4 Compatibility with Existing Bootstrap Action\n+\n+action `bootstrap` ที่ถูกใช้ปิด `#40` ไปแล้ว ต้องสามารถทำงานผ่าน selector resolver เดียวกันได้โดยไม่ regress:\n+\n+- branch bootstrap\n+- state transition\n+- current JSON success/error contract\n+\n+## 5. Non-Functional Requirements\n+\n+- ต้องคง backward compatibility กับ legacy numeric path เท่าที่ practical\n+- ต้องไม่ทำให้ interactive menu flow regress\n+- ต้องทำให้ external caller audit downstream target ได้ง่ายขึ้น\n+\n+## 6. Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- headless Luma actions รองรับ stable selector ที่ไม่ fragile กว่า numeric-only contract\n+- machine-readable response ระบุ resolved target ชัดเจน\n+- action `bootstrap` ใช้ resolver เดียวกับ actions อื่นได้\n+- legacy numeric path ยังใช้ต่อได้อย่างตั้งใจในฐานะ fallback\n+- มี tests ครอบคลุม selector resolution และ bootstrap compatibility\n+\n+## 7. Deferred Items\n+\n+สิ่งต่อไปนี้ยังไม่ควรถูกนับเป็น requirement ของรอบ `#84`:\n+\n+- rich structured payload เต็มรูปแบบเพื่อปิดทุก gap เดิมของ `#40`\n+- first-class issue creation parity ของ `#42`\n+- resumable guided workflow parity ของ `#41`\n+\n+ถ้าหลังทำ `#84` แล้วพบว่า `bootstrap` ยังมี gap เฉพาะตัวอยู่ ให้แตก follow-up issue ใหม่แยกต่างหาก\n+\n+## 8. Related\n+\n+- [Luma #40](https://github.com/oatrice/Luma/issues/40)\n+- [Luma #84](https://github.com/oatrice/Luma/issues/84)\n+- [Zenith #36](https://github.com/oatrice/Zenith/issues/36)"
}

Step-by-Step Implementation\n+\n+### Step 1: Update Input Parsing Logic\n+\n+- **Docs**: Update prompt message to indicate multi-select support\n+\n+- **Code**: Modify `action_add_issue` to parse comma-separated indices similar to `action_select_issue`\n+\n+- **Tests**: Add test case for comma-separated input\n+\n+### Step 2: Add Duplicate Prevention\n+\n+- **Docs**: Document duplicate checking behavior\n+\n+- **Code**: Check if issue already in active_issues before adding\n+\n+- **Tests**: Verify duplicate handling in tests\n+\n+### Step 3: Update Error Handling\n+\n+- **Docs**: Specify error messages for invalid indices\n+\n+- **Code**: Provide specific error messages for invalid indices\n+\n+- **Tests**: Test invalid input scenarios\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 3. Verification Plan\n+\n+*How will we verify success?*\n+\n+### Automated Tests\n+\n+- [x] Unit Tests: `tests/test_issue_actions.py` - `test_add_multiple_issues_comma_separated`\n+\n+- [x] Integration Tests: Manual testing with CLI\n+\n+### Manual Verification\n+\n+- [x] Test comma-separated input \"1,2,3\" adds multiple issues\n+\n+- [x] Test invalid indices show error messages\n+\n+- [x] Test duplicate selection is prevented\n+\n+- [x] Test single selection still works\n\\ No newline at end of file",
  "docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/analysis.md": "diff --git a/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/analysis.md b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/analysis.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..3510d05\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/analysis.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,228 @@\n+# Analysis Template\n+\n+## 📌 Feature Information\n+\n+| รายการ | รายละเอียด |\n+|--------|-----------|\n+| **Feature Name** | Fix Invalid Selection When Adding Multiple Issues |\n+| **Date** | 2026-04-29 |\n+| **Analyst** | Kilo |\n+| **Priority** | 🔴 High |\n+| **Status** | ✅ Approved |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 1. Requirement Analysis\n+\n+### 1.1 Problem Statement\n+\n+When attempting to add multiple issues to the current work session by typing '1,2,3' (with commas), the system shows 'Invalid selection'. The system only supported single issue selection, rejecting comma-separated input as invalid.\n+\n+### 1.2 User Stories\n+\n+| # | As a | I want to | So that |\n+|---|------|-----------|---------|\n+| 1 | Developer | add multiple issues with comma-separated input like \"1,2,3\" | I can efficiently select multiple issues at once without individual selections |\n+\n+### 1.3 Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [x] **AC1:** System accepts comma-separated input (e.g., \"1,2,3\") for adding multiple issues\n+- [x] **AC2:** Invalid indices are handled with appropriate error messages\n+- [x] **AC3:** Duplicate issue selections are prevented\n+- [x] **AC4:** Single issue selection (e.g., \"1\") continues to work\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 2. Feature Analysis\n+\n+### 2.1 User Flow\n+\n+```mermaid\n+flowchart TD\n+    A[Start Add Issue] --> B[Display available issues with numbers]\n+    B --> C[User enters comma-separated numbers]\n+    C --> D{Valid input?}\n+    D -->|Yes| E[Parse indices]\n+    D -->|No| F[Show error]\n+    E --> G{Valid indices?}\n+    G -->|Yes| H[Add issues, avoid duplicates]\n+    G -->|No| I[Show invalid index error]\n+    H --> J[Show success message]\n+    F --> B\n+    I --> B\n+    J --> K[End]\n+```\n+\n+### 2.2 Screen/Page Requirements\n+\n+| หน้าจอ | Actions | Components | UI Mock Status |\n+|--------|---------|------------|----------------|\n+| CLI Add Issue | Display issue list, accept multi-select input, show results | Issue list with indices, input prompt with multi-select hint | ✅ Done |\n+\n+### 2.3 Input/Output Specification\n+\n+#### Inputs\n+\n+| Field | Type | Required | Validation |\n+|-------|------|----------|------------|\n+| selection | string | ✅ | comma-separated integers, 1-based indices |\n+\n+#### Outputs\n+\n+| Field | Type | Description |\n+|-------|------|-------------|\n+| success | boolean | Whether issues were added successfully |\n+| added_issues | list | List of added issue numbers |\n+| errors | list | List of error messages for invalid inputs |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 3. Impact Analysis\n+\n+### 3.1 Affected Components\n+\n+| Component | Impact Level | Description |\n+|-----------|--------------|-------------|\n+| `luma_core/actions/issue_actions.py` - `action_add_issue` | 🔴 High | Modified to parse comma-separated input and support multi-select |\n+| `tests/test_issue_actions.py` | 🟡 Medium | Added test cases for comma-separated input |\n+\n+### 3.2 Breaking Changes\n+\n+- [ ] **BC1:** None - backward compatibility maintained\n+\n+### 3.3 Backward Compatibility Plan\n+\n+Single issue selection continues to work as before. No breaking changes.\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 4. Feasibility Analysis\n+\n+### 4.1 Technical Feasibility\n+\n+| คำถาม | คำตอบ | หมายเหตุ |\n+|-------|-------|----------|\n+| เทคโนโลยีรองรับหรือไม่? | ✅ | Python string parsing supports comma splitting |\n+| ทีมมี Skills เพียงพอหรือไม่? | ✅ | Basic Python development |\n+| Infrastructure รองรับหรือไม่? | ✅ | No additional infrastructure needed |\n+\n+### 4.2 Time Feasibility\n+\n+| ประเด็น | รายละเอียด |\n+|--------|-----------|\n+| **Estimated Effort** | 2 hours |\n+| **Deadline** | Completed |\n+| **Buffer Time** | N/A |\n+| **Feasible?** | ✅ |\n+\n+### 4.3 Budget Feasibility\n+\n+| รายการ | ค่าใช้จ่าย | หมายเหตุ |\n+|--------|-----------|----------|\n+| Development Time | Minimal | Existing codebase |\n+| **Total** | Low | |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 5. Security Analysis\n+\n+### 5.1 Sensitive Data\n+\n+| ข้อมูล | Sensitivity Level | Protection Method |\n+|--------|------------------|-------------------|\n+| User input | 🟢 Normal | Input validation |\n+\n+### 5.2 Attack Vectors\n+\n+| Vector | Risk Level | Mitigation |\n+|--------|-----------|------------|\n+| Invalid input | 🟢 Low | Input validation and error handling |\n+\n+### 5.3 Authentication & Authorization\n+\n+No authentication required for this CLI feature.\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 6. Performance & Scalability Analysis\n+\n+### 6.1 Performance Targets\n+\n+| Metric | Target | Current |\n+|--------|--------|---------|\n+| Input parsing time | < 100ms | N/A |\n+| Issue addition time | < 500ms | N/A |\n+\n+### 6.2 Scalability Plan\n+\n+| Scenario | Expected Users | Scaling Strategy |\n+|----------|---------------|------------------|\n+| Normal | 1 user | No scaling needed |\n+| Peak | 1 user | No scaling needed |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 7. Gap Analysis\n+\n+| ด้าน | As-Is (ปัจจุบัน) | To-Be (ต้องการ) | Gap |\n+|------|-----------------|-----------------|-----|\n+| Input parsing | Single integer only | Comma-separated integers | Implemented parsing logic |\n+| Multi-select | Not supported | Supported | Added multi-select capability |\n+| Error handling | Basic | Enhanced | Added invalid index and duplicate handling |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 8. Risk Analysis\n+\n+| Risk | Probability | Impact | Score | Mitigation Plan |\n+|------|-------------|--------|-------|-----------------|\n+| Input parsing errors | 🟡 Medium | 🟡 Medium | 4 | Comprehensive try/except with validation |\n+| Duplicate additions | 🟡 Medium | 🟢 Low | 2 | Added duplicate checking |\n+| Regression in single select | 🟢 Low | 🟡 Medium | 2 | Maintained backward compatibility |\n+\n+> **Risk Score:** Probability × Impact (High=3, Medium=2, Low=1)\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 9. Summary & Recommendations\n+\n+### 9.1 Analysis Summary\n+\n+| หมวด | Status | Key Findings |\n+|------|--------|--------------|\n+| Requirement | ✅ Clear | Well-defined issue with specific input format |\n+| Feature | ✅ Defined | CLI enhancement for better UX |\n+| Impact | 🟡 Medium | Code changes in core action, test additions |\n+| Feasibility | ✅ Feasible | Simple implementation, low risk |\n+| Security | ✅ Acceptable | No security concerns |\n+| Performance | ✅ Acceptable | Minimal performance impact |\n+| Risk | 🟡 Some Risks | Low to medium risks mitigated |\n+\n+### 9.2 Recommendations\n+\n+1. **Monitor usage:** Track how often multi-select is used vs single select\n+2. **Consider space-separated:** If users prefer spaces, could add support later\n+3. **Add more validation:** Consider maximum number of issues that can be added at once\n+\n+### 9.3 Next Steps\n+\n+- [x] Implementation completed\n+- [x] Tests added and passing\n+- [x] Manual verification done\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 📎 Appendix\n+\n+### Related Documents\n+\n+- Issue: https://github.com/oatrice/Luma/issues/90\n+- Implementation: `luma_core/actions/issue_actions.py` - `action_add_issue`\n+\n+### Sign-off\n+\n+| Role | Name | Date | Signature |\n+|------|------|------|-----------|\n+| Analyst | Kilo | 2026-04-29 | ✅ |\n+| Tech Lead | N/A | N/A | ⬜ |\n+| PM | N/A | N/A | ⬜ |\n\\ No newline at end of file",
  "docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/manual_verify.md": "diff --git a/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/manual_verify.md b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/manual_verify.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..759788b\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/manual_verify.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@\n+# Manual Verification Guide: Issue #90 - CLI Fix Invalid Selection When Adding Multiple Issues\n+\n+## Prerequisites\n+\n+- มี Luma environment พร้อม (Python 3.9+, GitHub CLI authenticated)\n+- มี GitHub project ที่มี Kanban และ issues ในสถานะ \"Ready\" หรือ \"In Progress\" อย่างน้อย 3-4 issues\n+- อยู่ใน CODING phase (หลังจาก select issue แล้ว)\n+- ใช้ real CLI หรือ headless mode สำหรับ testing\n+\n+## Verification Scenarios\n+\n+### 1. Scenario: Comma-separated Multiple Issues (Happy Path)\n+\n+**Addresses:** Core issue #90 - แก้ปัญหา \"Invalid selection\" เมื่อพิมพ์ comma-separated เช่น \"1,2,3\"\n+\n+**Steps:**\n+1. รัน Luma CLI และเลือก project\n+2. เลือก issue เพื่อเข้าสู่ CODING phase (ถ้ายังไม่ได้)\n+3. เลือก menu \"Add Issue to Current Work Session\"\n+4. เมื่อ prompt \"Select issue(s) to add:\" พิมพ์ \"1,2,3\"\n+5. Observe output\n+\n+**Verify:**\n+- ✅ แสดง success messages: \"✅ Added #X: Title\" สำหรับแต่ละ issue\n+- ✅ แสดง \"Active issues: #X, #Y, #Z\" รวม 3 issues\n+- ✅ ไม่มี error message\n+- ✅ State อัพเดทถูกต้อง (check ด้วย `luma state` หรือ .luma_state.json)\n+\n+### 2. Scenario: Space-separated Multiple Issues (Extended Support)\n+\n+**Addresses:** Recommendation สำหรับ space-separated support เช่น \"1 2 3\"\n+\n+**Steps:** เหมือน Scenario 1 แต่พิมพ์ \"1 2 3\" แทน \"1,2,3\"\n+\n+**Verify:** เหมือน Scenario 1 - เพิ่ม issues ได้และแสดง messages ถูกต้อง\n+\n+### 3. Scenario: Invalid Index Handling (Error Handling)\n+\n+**Addresses:** จัดการ invalid inputs อย่างเหมาะสม\n+\n+**Steps:**\n+1-4 เหมือน Scenario 1 แต่พิมพ์ \"1,99\" (สมมติมี issues แค่ 3-4 ตัว)\n+\n+**Verify:**\n+- ✅ แสดง error \"❌ Invalid index: 99\"\n+- ✅ แต่เพิ่ม issue ที่ valid (1) ได้ และแสดง success message\n+- ✅ Active issues เพิ่มแค่ 1 ตัว\n+\n+### 4. Scenario: Duplicate Prevention (Edge Case)\n+\n+**Addresses:** ป้องกันการเพิ่ม duplicate issues\n+\n+**Steps:**\n+1-2 เหมือน Scenario 1\n+3. เพิ่ม issue 1 ด้วย \"1\"\n+4. เลือก \"Add Issue\" อีกครั้ง และพิมพ์ \"1,2\"\n+\n+**Verify:**\n+- ✅ แสดง warning \"⚠️ #61 already active, skipping\" สำหรับ issue 1\n+- ✅ เพิ่มแค่ issue 2 และแสดง success message\n+- ✅ Active issues รวม 2 ตัว (1 เดิม + 2 ใหม่)\n+\n+### 5. Scenario: Single Issue Backward Compatibility (Regression Test)\n+\n+**Addresses:** ตรวจสอบ backward compatibility\n+\n+**Steps:** เหมือน Scenario 1 แต่พิมพ์ \"1\" (single digit)\n+\n+**Verify:**\n+- ✅ เพิ่ม issue 1 ได้ปกติ\n+- ✅ แสดง success message \"✅ Added #61: Title1\"\n+- ✅ Active issues อัพเดทถูกต้อง\n+\n+## Conclusion\n+\n+หากทุก scenario pass, implementation ถือว่าตอบโจทย์ issue #90 ครบถ้วนแล้ว และพร้อมสำหรับ production.\n\\ No newline at end of file",
  "docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/spec.md": "diff --git a/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/spec.md b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/spec.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..4dc4e2c\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/spec.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,105 @@\n+# Specification: Fix Invalid Selection When Adding Multiple Issues\n+\n+> **Status**: Approved\n+\n+> **Owner**: Kilo\n+\n+> **Dates**: Created: 2026-04-29 | Last Updated: 2026-04-29\n+\n+## 1. Context & Goal\n+\n+*Why are we building this? What is the problem statement?*\n+\n+### Problem\n+\n+Users cannot efficiently add multiple issues to their current work session. When attempting to enter comma-separated issue numbers like \"1,2,3\", the system rejects the input as \"Invalid selection\", forcing users to add issues one at a time.\n+\n+### Goal\n+\n+Enable comma-separated input for adding multiple issues simultaneously, improving user efficiency and matching the behavior of issue selection.\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 2. User Journey & Requirements\n+\n+*What should the user experience?*\n+\n+### User Story\n+\n+As a **developer working on multiple related issues**, I want to **add several issues at once using comma-separated input**, so that **I can quickly build my work session without repetitive individual selections**.\n+\n+### Functional Requirements\n+\n+- [x] Support comma-separated input (e.g., \"1,2,3\") for adding multiple issues\n+\n+- [x] Maintain backward compatibility with single issue selection\n+\n+- [x] Prevent duplicate issue additions\n+\n+- [x] Provide clear error messages for invalid inputs\n+\n+- [x] Update UI prompts to indicate multi-select capability\n+\n+### Non-Functional Requirements\n+\n+- [x] Performance: Input parsing should be fast (<100ms)\n+\n+- [x] Reliability: Invalid inputs should not crash the system\n+\n+- [x] Usability: Error messages should be clear and actionable\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 3. Specification by Example (SBE)\n+\n+*Concrete examples of behavior.*\n+\n+### Scenario: Add Multiple Issues Successfully\n+\n+**Given** user is in CODING phase\n+\n+**When** user chooses \"Add Issue to Current Work Session\"\n+\n+**And** enters \"1,2,3\"\n+\n+**Then** issues 1, 2, and 3 are added to active issues\n+\n+**And** success messages are displayed\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| Input Format | Issues Added | User Feedback |\n+|--------------|--------------|---------------|\n+| \"1,2,3\" | 3 issues | \"✅ Added #61: Title1\" etc. |\n+| \"1\" | 1 issue | \"✅ Added #61: Title1\" |\n+| \" 1 , 2 \" | 2 issues | Success messages |\n+\n+### Scenario: Handle Invalid Inputs\n+\n+**Given** user is prompted for issue selection\n+\n+**When** user enters invalid input\n+\n+**Then** system shows appropriate error messages\n+\n+**And** no invalid issues are added\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| Invalid Input | Error Response |\n+|---------------|----------------|\n+| \"1,99\" | \"❌ Invalid index: 99\" |\n+| \"abc\" | \"❌ Invalid selection\" |\n+| \"0\" | \"❌ Invalid index: 0\" |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## 4. Constraints & Risks\n+\n+*What should we watch out for?*\n+\n+- Constraint: Must maintain backward compatibility\n+\n+- Risk: Parsing errors could cause unexpected behavior\n+\n+- Risk: Performance impact from parsing multiple indices\n\\ No newline at end of file",

Handle headless_args if provided\n     if headless_args:\n@@ -77,7 +85,7 @@ def action_create_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict, title: str = None, body\n             else:\n                 if not headless:\n                     print(\"   ⚠️ Could not add to Kanban (may need manual addition)\")\n-        \n+\n         if not headless:\n             print(f\"   ✅ Created: {result['url']}\")\n         return result\n@@ -86,6 +94,7 @@ def action_create_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict, title: str = None, body\n             print(f\"   ❌ Failed: {result.get('error')}\")\n         return result\n \n+\n def action_select_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n     \"\"\"Select an issue from Kanban (Ready or In Progress)\"\"\"\n     print(\"\\n💡 เช็ค gh cli, Roadmap.md ว่าต้องทำ issue ไหนต่อ\")\n@@ -115,7 +124,9 @@ def action_select_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n \n     # Auto-sync CLOSED issues to Roadmap.md (silent background sync)\n     try:\n-        done_statuses = {_status_key(s) for s in workflow.get(\"done_statuses\", [\"Done\", \"Closed\"])}\n+        done_statuses = {\n+            _status_key(s) for s in workflow.get(\"done_statuses\", [\"Done\", \"Closed\"])\n+        }\n         closed_issue_nums = [\n             c.issue_number for c in all_cards if _status_key(c.status) in done_statuses\n         ]\n@@ -171,13 +182,13 @@ def action_select_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n                 print(f\"❌ Invalid index: {idx + 1}\")\n         if not selected_cards:\n             return False\n-        \n+\n         # Detect cross-repo links from selected issue bodies\n         cross_repo_links = []\n         for card in selected_cards:\n             if card.body:\n                 cross_repo_links.extend(detect_zenith_issues_from_text(card.body))\n-        \n+\n         # Remove duplicates\n         seen = set()\n         unique_links = []\n@@ -186,12 +197,12 @@ def action_select_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n             if key not in seen:\n                 unique_links.append(link)\n                 seen.add(key)\n-        \n+\n         if unique_links:\n             print(f\"\\n🔗 Cross-Repo Links Detected ({len(unique_links)}):\")\n             for link in unique_links:\n                 print(f\"   • {link.repo}#{link.issue_number} → {link.url}\")\n-            \n+\n             # Store in state for later PR creation\n             state.context[\"cross_repo_links\"] = [\n                 {\n@@ -203,12 +214,13 @@ def action_select_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n                 for link in unique_links\n             ]\n             print(\"   💡 Links will be auto-included when creating PR\")\n-            \n+\n         return _start_issues(state, selected_cards, project)\n     except (ValueError, IndexError):\n         print(\"❌ Invalid input. Please enter numbers.\")\n         return False\n \n+\n def action_add_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n     \"\"\"Add an issue to the current active issues (mid-work)\"\"\"\n     if state.phase not in [WorkflowPhase.CODING, WorkflowPhase.PREFLIGHT]:\n@@ -232,36 +244,51 @@ def action_add_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n     for i, card in enumerate(selectable, 1):\n         print(f\"  [{i}] #{card.issue_number}: {card.title[:50]} ({card.status})\")\n     print(\"  [0] Cancel\")\n+    print(\"  ℹ️  Comma-separated or space-separated for multi-select (e.g. 1,3 or 1 3)\")\n \n-    choice = safe_input(\"\\nSelect issue to add: \").strip()\n+    choice = safe_input(\"\\nSelect issue(s) to add: \").strip()\n     if choice == \"0\":\n         return False\n \n+    # Parse multi-select (e.g. \"1,3\", \"1 3\", or \"1\")\n     try:\n-        idx = int(choice) - 1\n-        if 0 <= idx < len(selectable):\n-            card = selectable[idx]\n-            new_issue = IssueData(\n-                number=card.issue_number,\n-                title=card.title,\n-                html_url=card.url,\n-                body=card.body,\n-                project_item_id=card.item_id,\n-                repository=card.repository,\n-            )\n-            state.active_issues.append(new_issue)\n-            print(f\"✅ Added #{card.issue_number}: {card.title[:40]}\")\n+        if \",\" in choice:\n+            indices = [int(x.strip()) - 1 for x in choice.split(\",\")]\n+        else:\n+            indices = [int(x.strip()) - 1 for x in choice.split()]\n+        added_count = 0\n+        for idx in indices:\n+            if 0 <= idx < len(selectable):\n+                card = selectable[idx]\n+                # Check if already active to avoid duplicates\n+                if any(i.number == card.issue_number for i in state.active_issues):\n+                    print(f\"⚠️  #{card.issue_number} already active, skipping\")\n+                    continue\n+                new_issue = IssueData(\n+                    number=card.issue_number,\n+                    title=card.title,\n+                    html_url=card.url,\n+                    body=card.body,\n+                    project_item_id=card.item_id,\n+                    repository=card.repository,\n+                )\n+                state.active_issues.append(new_issue)\n+                print(f\"✅ Added #{card.issue_number}: {card.title[:40]}\")\n+                added_count += 1\n+\n+                # Sync Kanban\n+                if card.item_id and project.get(\"kanban_id\"):\n+                    sync_kanban_on_action(\n+                        \"select_issue\",\n+                        project[\"kanban_id\"],\n+                        card.item_id,\n+                    )\n+            else:\n+                print(f\"❌ Invalid index: {idx + 1}\")\n+        if added_count > 0:\n             print(\n                 f\"   Active issues: {', '.join(f'#{i.number}' for i in state.active_issues)}\"\n             )\n-\n-            # Sort indices descending to avoid index shift when popping\n+        indices_sorted = sorted(set(indices), reverse=True)\n+\n+        # Count valid indices to check if removal would leave at least 1 issue\n+        valid_indices = [idx for idx in indices_sorted if 0 <= idx < len(state.active_issues)]\n+        if len(state.active_issues) - len(valid_indices) < 1:\n+            print(f\"Cannot remove: need at least 1 active issue remaining.\")\n+            return False\n+\n+        removed_count = 0\n+        for idx in indices_sorted:\n+            if 0 <= idx < len(state.active_issues):\n+                removed = state.active_issues.pop(idx)\n+                print(f\"Removed #{removed.number}: {removed.title[:40]}\")\n+                removed_count += 1\n+            else:\n+                print(f\"Invalid index: {idx + 1}\")\n+\n+        if removed_count > 0:\n             print(\n                 f\"   Remaining: {', '.join(f'#{i.number}' for i in state.active_issues)}\"\n             )\n@@ -297,12 +347,13 @@ def action_remove_issue(state: LumaState, project: dict) -> bool:\n     except ValueError:\n         pass\n \n-    print(\"❌ Invalid selection\")\n+    print(\"Invalid selection\")\n     return False\n \n+\n def action_view_kanban(project: dict):\n     \"\"\"View Kanban status\"\"\"\n-    print(f\"\\n📊 Fetching {project['name']} Kanban...\")\n+    print(f\"\\n Fetching {project['name']} Kanban...\")\n     workflow = get_status_workflow(project)\n     board_priority = _status_priority(workflow.get(\"board_order\", []))\n \n@@ -329,12 +380,15 @@ def action_view_kanban(project: dict):\n         for card in cards_by_status[status]:\n             print(f\"  #{card.issue_number}: {card.title[:65]}\")\n \n+\n def action_list_active_issues(project: dict):\n     \"\"\"List all active issues (Backlog, Ready, In Progress)\"\"\"\n     print(f\"\\n📋 Fetching Active Issues for {project['name']}...\")\n     workflow = get_status_workflow(project)\n     #   "tests/test_issue_actions.py": "diff --git a/tests/test_issue_actions.py b/tests/test_issue_actions.py\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..311b60f\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/tests/test_issue_actions.py\n@@ -0,0 +1,331 @@\n+import unittest\n+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch\n+from luma_core.actions.issue_actions import action_add_issue, action_remove_issue\n+from luma_core.state_manager import LumaState\n+from luma_core.state_manager import WorkflowPhase, IssueData\n+\n+\n+class MockCard:\n+    def __init__(self, issue_number, title, url, body, item_id, repository, status):\n+        self.issue_number = issue_number\n+        self.title = title\n+        self.url = url\n+        self.body = body\n+        self.item_id = item_id\n+        self.repository = repository\n+        self.status = status\n+\n+\n+class TestActionAddIssue(unittest.TestCase):\n+    def test_add_multiple_issues_space_separated(self):\n+        # Setup state\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = []  # empty\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock selectable cards\n+        selectable = [\n+            MockCard(61, \"Title1\", \"url1\", \"body1\", \"item1\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+            MockCard(62, \"Title2\", \"url2\", \"body2\", \"item2\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+            MockCard(63, \"Title3\", \"url3\", \"body3\", \"item3\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        # Mock inputs: \"1 2 3\"\n+        with patch(\n+            \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.fetch_kanban_cards\", return_value=[]\n+        ) as mock_fetch:\n+            with patch(\n+                \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions._get_selectable_cards\",\n+                return_value=selectable,\n+            ) as mock_select:\n+                with patch(\n+                    \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"1 2 3\"\n+                ) as mock_input:\n+                    with patch(\n+                        \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.sync_kanban_on_action\"\n+                    ) as mock_sync:\n+                        result = action_add_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 3)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 61)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[1].number, 62)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[2].number, 63)\n+\n+        # Verify sync called 3 times\n+        self.assertEqual(mock_sync.call_count, 3)\n+\n+    def test_add_single_issue_backward_compatibility(self):\n+        # Setup state\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = []  # empty\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock selectable cards\n+        selectable = [\n+            MockCard(61, \"Title1\", \"url1\", \"body1\", \"item1\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        # Mock inputs: \"1\"\n+        with patch(\n+            \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.fetch_kanban_cards\", return_value=[]\n+        ) as mock_fetch:\n+            with patch(\n+                \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions._get_selectable_cards\",\n+                return_value=selectable,\n+            ) as mock_select:\n+                with patch(\n+                    \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"1\"\n+                ) as mock_input:\n+                    with patch(\n+                        \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.sync_kanban_on_action\"\n+                    ) as mock_sync:\n+                        result = action_add_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 1)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 61)\n+\n+        # Verify sync called 1 time\n+        self.assertEqual(mock_sync.call_count, 1)\n+\n+    def test_add_issue_invalid_input(self):\n+        # Setup state\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = []  # empty\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock selectable cards\n+        selectable = [\n+            MockCard(61, \"Title1\", \"url1\", \"body1\", \"item1\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        # Mock inputs: \"abc\"\n+        with patch(\n+            \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.fetch_kanban_cards\", return_value=[]\n+        ) as mock_fetch:\n+            with patch(\n+                \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions._get_selectable_cards\",\n+                return_value=selectable,\n+            ) as mock_select:\n+                with patch(\n+                    \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"abc\"\n+                ) as mock_input:\n+                    with patch(\n+                        \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.sync_kanban_on_action\"\n+                    ) as mock_sync:\n+                        result = action_add_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertFalse(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 0)\n+\n+        # Verify sync not called\n+        self.assertEqual(mock_sync.call_count, 0)\n+\n+    def test_add_issue_duplicate_prevention(self):\n+        # Setup state with existing issue\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        existing_issue = IssueData(\n+            number=61,\n+            title=\"Title1\",\n+            html_url=\"url1\",\n+            body=\"body1\",\n+            project_item_id=\"item1\",\n+            repository=\"repo\",\n+        )\n+        state.active_issues = [existing_issue]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock selectable cards\n+        selectable = [\n+            MockCard(61, \"Title1\", \"url1\", \"body1\", \"item1\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+            MockCard(62, \"Title2\", \"url2\", \"body2\", \"item2\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        # Mock inputs: \"1,2\"\n+        with patch(\n+            \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.fetch_kanban_cards\", return_value=[]\n+        ) as mock_fetch:\n+            with patch(\n+                \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions._get_selectable_cards\",\n+                return_value=selectable,\n+            ) as mock_select:\n+                with patch(\n+                    \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"1,2\"\n+                ) as mock_input:\n+                    with patch(\n+                        \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.sync_kanban_on_action\"\n+                    ) as mock_sync:\n+                        result = action_add_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 2)  # Only added 1 more\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 61)  # Existing\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[1].number, 62)  # New\n+\n+        # Verify sync called 1 time (only for new issue)\n+        self.assertEqual(mock_sync.call_count, 1)\n+\n+    def test_add_multiple_issues_comma_separated(self):\n+        # Setup state\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = []  # empty\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock selectable cards\n+        selectable = [\n+            MockCard(61, \"Title1\", \"url1\", \"body1\", \"item1\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+            MockCard(62, \"Title2\", \"url2\", \"body2\", \"item2\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+            MockCard(63, \"Title3\", \"url3\", \"body3\", \"item3\", \"repo\", \"Ready\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        # Mock inputs: \"1,2,3\"\n+        with patch(\n+            \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.fetch_kanban_cards\", return_value=[]\n+        ) as mock_fetch:\n+            with patch(\n+                \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions._get_selectable_cards\",\n+                return_value=selectable,\n+            ) as mock_select:\n+                with patch(\n+                    \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"1,2,3\"\n+                ) as mock_input:\n+                    with patch(\n+                        \"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.sync_kanban_on_action\"\n+                    ) as mock_sync:\n+                        result = action_add_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 3)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 61)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[1].number, 62)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[2].number, 63)\n+\n+        # Verify sync called 3 times\n+        self.assertEqual(mock_sync.call_count, 3)\n+\n+class TestActionRemoveIssue(unittest.TestCase):\n+    def test_remove_multiple_issues_comma_separated(self):\n+        \"\"\"Test removing multiple issues with comma-separated input (e.g. '3,4')\"\"\"\n+        # Setup state with 4 active issues\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = [\n+            IssueData(84, \"Primary Issue\", \"url84\", \"body84\", \"item84\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(90, \"Issue 90\", \"url90\", \"body90\", \"item90\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(17, \"Issue 17\", \"url17\", \"body17\", \"item17\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(18, \"Issue 18\", \"url18\", \"body18\", \"item18\", \"repo\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock input: \"3,4\" to remove issues at index 2 and 3 (#17 and #18)\n+        with patch(\"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"3,4\"):\n+            result = action_remove_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 2)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 84)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[1].number, 90)\n+\n+    def test_remove_multiple_issues_space_separated(self):\n+        \"\"\"Test removing multiple issues with space-separated input (e.g. '2 3')\"\"\"\n+        # Setup state with 3 active issues\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = [\n+            IssueData(84, \"Primary Issue\", \"url84\", \"body84\", \"item84\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(90, \"Issue 90\", \"url90\", \"body90\", \"item90\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(17, \"Issue 17\", \"url17\", \"body17\", \"item17\", \"repo\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock input: \"2 3\" to remove issues at index 1 and 2 (#90 and #17)\n+        with patch(\"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"2 3\"):\n+            result = action_remove_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 1)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 84)\n+\n+    def test_remove_single_issue_backward_compatibility(self):\n+        \"\"\"Test removing single issue still works (backward compatibility)\"\"\"\n+        # Setup state with 2 active issues\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = [\n+            IssueData(84, \"Primary Issue\", \"url84\", \"body84\", \"item84\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(90, \"Issue 90\", \"url90\", \"body90\", \"item90\", \"repo\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock input: \"2\" to remove issue at index 1 (#90)\n+        with patch(\"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"2\"):\n+            result = action_remove_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 1)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 84)\n+\n+    def test_remove_issue_invalid_input(self):\n+        \"\"\"Test invalid input handling\"\"\"\n+        # Setup state with 2 active issues\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = [\n+            IssueData(84, \"Primary Issue\", \"url84\", \"body84\", \"item84\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(90, \"Issue 90\", \"url90\", \"body90\", \"item90\", \"repo\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock input: \"abc\" (invalid)\n+        with patch(\"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"abc\"):\n+            result = action_remove_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert\n+        self.assertFalse(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 2)  # No change\n+\n+    def test_remove_issue_invalid_index(self):\n+        \"\"\"Test invalid index handling (partial success)\"\"\"\n+        # Setup state with 3 active issues\n+        state = LumaState()\n+        state.phase = WorkflowPhase.CODING\n+        state.active_issues = [\n+            IssueData(84, \"Primary Issue\", \"url84\", \"body84\", \"item84\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(90, \"Issue 90\", \"url90\", \"body90\", \"item90\", \"repo\"),\n+            IssueData(17, \"Issue 17\", \"url17\", \"body17\", \"item17\", \"repo\"),\n+        ]\n+\n+        project = {\"kanban_number\": 1, \"kanban_id\": \"kanban_id\"}\n+\n+        # Mock input: \"2,99\" (99 is invalid but 2 is valid)\n+        with patch(\"luma_core.actions.issue_actions.safe_input\", return_value=\"2,99\"):\n+            result = action_remove_issue(state, project)\n+\n+        # Assert - should remove issue 2 and report invalid for 99\n+        self.assertTrue(result)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(state.active_issues), 2)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[0].number, 84)\n+        self.assertEqual(state.active_issues[1].number, 17)",

a/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/sbe.md b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/sbe.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..74b2c79\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/docs/features/26_issue-90_cli-fix-invalid-selection-when-adding-multiple-issues-dc3001bc/sbe.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,80 @@\n+# SBE (Specification by Example) Template\n+\n+> 📅 Created: 2026-04-29\n+\n+> 🔗 Issue: https://github.com/oatrice/Luma/issues/90\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Feature: Support Comma-Separated Multiple Issue Selection in Add Issue\n+\n+The system should allow users to add multiple issues to the current work session by entering comma-separated issue numbers (e.g., \"1,2,3\").\n+\n+### Scenario: Successful Addition of Multiple Issues\n+\n+**Given** the user is in the CODING phase with an active issue\n+\n+**When** the user selects \"Add Issue to Current Work Session\"\n+\n+**And** enters \"1,2,3\" when prompted for issue selection\n+\n+**Then** issues 1, 2, and 3 are added to the active issues list\n+\n+**And** a success message is displayed for each added issue\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| Input | Expected Issues Added | Success Message |\n+|-------|-----------------------|-----------------|\n+| \"1,2,3\" | Issues 1, 2, 3 | \"✅ Added #61: Title1\", \"✅ Added #62: Title2\", \"✅ Added #63: Title3\" |\n+| \"1\" | Issue 1 | \"✅ Added #61: Title1\" |\n+| \"2,4\" | Issues 2, 4 | \"✅ Added #62: Title2\", \"✅ Added #64: Title4\" |\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Scenario: Invalid Index Handling\n+\n+**Given** the user is prompted to select issues\n+\n+**When** the user enters an invalid index like \"1,99\"\n+\n+**Then** valid issues are added and invalid indices are reported\n+\n+**And** an error message is shown for invalid indices\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| Input | Valid Issues Added | Error Message |\n+|-------|-------------------|---------------|\n+| \"1,99\" | Issue 1 | \"❌ Invalid index: 99\" |\n+| \"0,1\" | None | \"❌ Invalid index: 0\" |\n+| \"abc,1\" | Issue 1 | \"❌ Invalid selection\" |\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Scenario: Duplicate Issue Prevention\n+\n+**Given** issue 1 is already active\n+\n+**When** the user enters \"1,2\"\n+\n+**Then** issue 1 is skipped with a warning\n+\n+**And** issue 2 is added successfully\n+\n+#### Examples\n+\n+| Input | Already Active | Added | Skipped | Messages |\n+|-------|----------------|-------|---------|----------|\n+| \"1,2\" | Issue 1 | Issue 2 | Issue 1 | \"⚠️ #61 already active, skipping\", \"✅ Added #62: Title2\" |\n+| \"3,3\" | None | Issue 3 | None | \"✅ Added #63: Title3\" |\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+- Indices are 1-based corresponding to the displayed list\n+\n+- Single issue selection continues to work as before\n+\n+- Comma-separated parsing handles whitespace around commas\n\\ No newline at end of file"
}

If the code looks correct, output ONLY 'PASS'. Otherwise, explain the fix.
